[EVENT: SYR113217]

Actor2: Military Forces of Syria (2000-2024) [State forces]

Sources: SOHR [Other]

A Hezbollah-affiliated gas station near Aabel village was hit by missiles and it remains unclear whether Israeli or regime missiles hit the location. The attack resulted in the killing of an unknown number of individuals in addition to injuring four. Unknown fatalities coded to 10 and split as follows: 5 Al Dab'a, 5 Aabel. 5 fatalities.
